## Files

| File | Purpose |
| --- | --- |
| `next_agent_handoff.md` | **The durable handoff.** Start every session here. Holds the current task, the open questions, source gaps, and the ready-to-run initial task message for the next agent. |
| `idea_register.md` | Full dossiers for every retained candidate (mechanism, observables, rule sketch, falsifiers, evidence status). |
| `ranked_shortlist.md` | Rubric-scored ranking with hard-stop flags, weights, and per-score justification. |
| `source_ledger.md` | Verified citations (ID, full reference, URL/DOI, what each source supports, verification method, caveats). |
| `validation_handoffs.md` | Theory-to-validation specifications for shortlisted candidates. Specification only — never executed here. |
| `rejected_and_parked.md` | Rejected/parked candidates with reasons, plus the known-baseline list, to prevent rediscovery. |
| `cycle_notes.md` | Cycle log, the mandatory three-pass task-framing loop, and the change history of the artifacts. |

## Working agreements for future sessions

1. **Read `next_agent_handoff.md` first**, then the register and shortlist, before generating anything new.
2. **Preserve prior work.** Add to the register; do not silently delete, relabel, or renumber existing IDs.
3. **ID discipline.** Ideas are `IDEA-0NN` (next free: `IDEA-017`; `IDEA-016` is **reserved and unused**), sources are `S-0NN` (next free: `S-458`; **only** `S-210 … S-219` is **reserved and unused** — `S-281 … S-290` are **live entries**, the ten Cycle-58 document-record entries, and the corpus is **447 entries of which 446 are verifiable**, S-290 being struck), validation specs are `V-0NN` (next free: `V-013`; `V-011` is **reserved and unused**, `V-010` is written and marked **never-to-execute**, and `V-012` is written and **executable** — the register's first *state-free* specification), rejections are `R-0NN` (next free: `R-076`), parkings are `P-0NN` (next free: `P-015`; `P-013` is **reserved and unused**), source gaps are `G-NN` (next free: `G-39`; `G-17` and `G-26` were skipped by the sequence and have never been used), and cycles are `Cycle N` (next: **Cycle 580**).
4. **Never invent a citation.** Every external claim in a dossier points to an `S-0NN` entry in the source ledger, and every ledger entry records how it was verified. If a claim cannot be sourced, mark it as an unresolved conjecture in the dossier.
5. **Scores are research-priority weights, not success probabilities.** No numerical probability of profit is attached to any candidate; the rubric score is a transparent weighted total out of 100.
6. **Theory only.** No backtests, no data acquisition or processing, no executable strategy code, no performance metrics, no workflow/test/infra edits, no cross-repository work. Unresolvable questions are documented and handed off, not silently relaxed.
7. **Every session ends with an updated handoff** naming the best next research task, the highest-value unresolved theoretical questions, and the next mechanism families to investigate.
